中砂 (1560 TT)  Trading Buy

先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，投資建議
給 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，中
砂持續擴充鑽石碟的產能，帶動獲利、評價提升，投資建議給
Trading Buy 。  
中砂 2Q26 獲利大幅年增： 中砂 2Q26 營收 24.93 億元，
QoQ+8.70%/YoY+17.87% ；稅後純益 5.23 億元，
QoQ+19.94%/YoY+96.49% ；稅後 EPS 3.46 元。 2Q26 業外利益
包含投資獲利。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況： ABU：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂
輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於 2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨
持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出貨。
SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望： 傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ：2026 年將
受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目標營收較 2025 雙位
數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐
季持續增加，美系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率
及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ：2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，
高階產品組合比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。  
群益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元：群
益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 101.87 億元 /135.79 億元，
YoY+25.00%/+33.30% ；稅後純益 21.97 億元 /31.87 億元，
YoY+61.48%/+45.07% ；稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 23 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
E：(Environmental) 。中砂秉持企業經營須兼顧環境保護之理念，持續落實環境
友善管理策略與措施。國際永續趨勢與國家 2050 淨零路徑推動下，中砂透過
執行組織型溫室氣體盤查、產品碳足跡盤查及導入循環經濟專案，循序漸進達
成減碳目標。  
此外，中砂重視氣候變遷伴隨之風險與機會，亦將氣候行動納入公司風險管理
與營運策略，積極研擬因應策略及應變措施；對於水資源、空氣污染、廢棄物
等環境議題，更推行各項管理與減量方案，朝向永續經營之願景邁進。  
S： (Social) 。中砂長期深耕地方社區創生與投入藝文教育與活動，使參與社會
公益成為中砂的企業文化。秉持著「共好：你好、我好、大家好」的經營理念
及懷著「知恩、感恩、報恩」的心，中砂持續與當地縣市政府、學校、社福機
構團體與自身基金會合作，攜手保留珍貴產業文化，提倡書法教育，為當地社
區帶來實質性的協助與回饋，發揮企業正向影響力。  
中砂座落於台灣著名的陶瓷之鄉 — 鶯歌，期望與鄉里共榮，為社區民眾打造友
善的生活環境，因此長期參與社區關懷與愛鄉感恩公益，連續多年贊助新北市
鶯歌區西鶯里、中湖里的敬老活動與銀髮族共餐活動，並致贈年節禮盒及物資
予鄰里長輩。近年中砂更與民間公益培訓單位合作，透過「學生提案，企業協
力」的方式，與公益團體合作推出聯名禮盒、愛心送餐等創新專案。除了達到
支援公益團體的目的外，中砂更將聯名禮盒分送給鶯歌的鄰里長輩，持續傳遞
公益善心。  
G： (Governance) 。企業永續發展的關鍵建基在健全且完善的公司治理架構上，
使得強化董事會監督管理職能成為中砂精進公 司治理的核心目標。除了保障股
東權益外，中砂更積極建構具備應對傳統及氣候變遷、資訊安全等新興風險的
管理機制，透過完整的鑑別、評估及擬定因應措施的流程，降低潛在風險對公
司營運之衝擊。在持續提升營運韌性的同時，也為利害關係人創造更大的價值。
公司簡介：  
中國砂輪股份有限公司成立於 07/1964 ，總部位於鶯歌，主要從事生產及銷售
砂輪、鑽石碟、再生晶圓等研磨工具。  
公司早期產品以應用於石材加工的傳統砂輪為主，後因取得鑽石碟技術的專利
授權及台系大客戶供應鏈在地化策略，以鑽石碟產品切入半導體產業領域。
2020 年，公司再生晶圓事業部竹科廠於竹南科 學園區落成啟用量產，成為全球
前三大再生晶圓供應商。  
再生晶圓主要台灣同業包含辛耘 (3583 TT) 、昇陽半導體 (8028 TT) ；鑽石碟為
3M(MMM US) 、Abrasive Technology 、Asahi(4216 JP) 、Seasol( 韓 )等。中砂大客
戶包含台系晶圓代工廠、美系記憶體廠等。  
中砂自 04/2025 開始認列日本三井金屬礦業集團旗下的兩家公司 MKS 與 MGT，
歸類在傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) 。  
營收占比：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ，毛利率 20~25% 。用於工具機、機械，近期還有包含半
導體 (碳化矽 )的產業。

3 群益投顧 
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ，毛利率 45% 以上。用於成熟製程、先進製程、先進封裝。
單一客戶營收占比超過 54% 。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ，毛利率 30~35% 。用於半導體製程，單價約 10~20 美金。

資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況：  
ABU ：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於
2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出
貨。 SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
2026 年 CAPEX 與擴產計畫：  
中砂 2026 年 CAPEX 約 15 億元， 4Q26 鑽石碟月產能預計增加至 7.0 萬顆、
4Q27 增加至 9.5 萬顆； 4Q26 12 吋再生晶圓月產能將增加至 40 萬片， 8 吋約
15 萬片，共 55 萬片。  
資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ： 2026 年將受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目
標營收較 2025 雙位數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐季持續增加，美
系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ： 2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，高階產品組合
比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

玻璃陶瓷–電子材料  
富喬(1815.TT) 
AI 材料升級趨勢確立，高階玻纖布開啟成長新局

富喬為台灣少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方能力的玻纖布廠。2H26 
受惠二代低介電布放量、報價調漲及良率改善，營收與 獲利可望逐季創
高；進入 2027 年後，獲利成長動能將由高階產品占比提升與新增產能
貢獻。台灣廠持續將既有產能轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起分
批將 E 布產能升級至二代布，泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐步投產，承接
客戶對非中國高階產能的需求，加上低膨脹布開始導入，產品組合將進
一步往高階化發展。研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 
5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後淨利分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富喬
為日系龍頭以外少數具備垂直整合與自有配方能力的供應商，以  2027 
年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買進
投資評等。

1. 規格升級推動產品組合高階化 
AI 伺服器與高速交換器的傳輸速率持續提升 ， 板材由 M7、M8 向 M9 以
上升級，玻纖布同步由 E-glass 轉向一代、二代低介電布。石英布因鑽
孔與除膠渣良率受限而導入遞延，終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布，延
長其產品週期。研究部預估富喬二代低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%。

2. 既有機台重新配置，毛利率大幅跳升 
玻纖布織布機由豐田獨家供應 ， 新機交期已排至 2031 年，2027 年以前
產業新增供給主要仰賴既有機台重新配置 。 富喬現有約 920 台織機 ， 憑
藉紗、布垂直整合，可在無須大幅增加資本支出下將既有產能轉向二代
低介電布 ， 帶動毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%， 升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 
2027 年的 54.8%。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
富喬工業（1815 TT）成立於 1999 年，為台灣少數具備玻璃纖維紗至玻璃纖
維布垂直整合能力的材料廠，主要產品涵蓋電子級、工業級玻纖紗及電子級玻
纖布，其中電子級玻纖布為銅箔基板（CCL）與印刷電路板（PCB）的關鍵上游
材料。公司目前主要生產基地位於雲林斗六、虎尾及中國東莞，並已布局泰國
新產能，以強化非中國供應鏈配置。相較單純織布廠，富喬具備自有玻纖紗生
產與配方研發能力，可依終端規格變化調整紗種與產品組合。近年受惠 AI 伺
服器、高速交換器等應用帶動 PCB 材料規格持續升級，富喬營運重心逐步由
傳統 E-glass 轉向低介電等高階電子級材料。

玻纖布產業介紹 
玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最上游，製程橫跨玻璃熔煉與紡織兩個領域。玻
纖原料經窯爐熔融後 ， 由鉑銠漏板拉製成單絲 ， 再經上漿 、 集束與加撚形成電
子紗 ， 後續以織機織成玻纖布 ， 並經開纖及矽烷偶聯劑等後處理後 ， 交由 CCL 
廠含浸樹脂製成膠片，再與銅箔壓合成銅箔基板，最終應用於 PCB 或載板。 
玻纖布規格主要可由織物型號 、 紗徑與玻璃配方三個面向區分 。 以織物型號而
言，7628 屬厚布，基重約 210 g/m²，2116、1080 依序變薄，10 系列則屬超
薄布；若依紗徑區分，粗紗單纖直徑約 9 至 13 微米，細紗則降至約 4 至 
9 微米，隨紗徑變細，斷絲風險與製程難度同步提高，織機亦須降速，超薄布
生產效率僅約厚布的二分之一至三分之一 。 另一方面 ， 玻璃配方則直接決定材
料電性 ， 介電損耗亦隨材料世代持續下降 。 隨高速傳輸規格提升 ，玻纖與樹脂
之間的介電差異會放大訊號時延與織紋效應 ， 因此傳輸速率每提升一個世代 ，
材料亦須同步升級。 
除玻璃配方外，織布後的開纖與表面處理亦是高階玻纖布性能的關鍵。即使
CCL 廠商採用相同原料，不同玻纖布廠的產品在電性、耐熱性與良率上的表現
仍可能存在顯著差異，也使高階玻纖布認證週期通常長達兩年以上。就材料成
本結構而言，玻纖布占傳統 CCL 材料成本約 25% 至 40%，若升級至石英布，
占比可提高至約六成；隨高階 PCB 對 CCL 規格與用量提升，CCL 占 PCB 用
料成本比重亦由過去三至四成提升至五成以上。玻纖布因此已由過去的通用材
料，逐步轉變為影響高速板材性能、成本與供應穩定性的關鍵材料。

富喬為台灣少數具備玻纖紗、布
垂直整合與自有配方能力的材料
廠，近年受惠 AI 帶動高階 PCB 
材料升級 ， 營運重心正由傳統 E-
glass 轉向低介電玻纖布，並同
步布局泰國非中國產能。

玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最
上游 ， 製程與認證門檻高 ， 且隨高
速傳輸推升材料規格升級，其成
本占比與對板材性能的重要性同
步提升，已由通用材料轉為高階  
PCB 的關鍵材料。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

AI 世代推升高速材料規格持續升級 
AI 基礎建設對材料的需求，並非僅來自伺服器出貨量成長，更重要的是運算
密度與傳輸速率持續提升，帶動 PCB 材料規格同步升級。相較傳統伺服器約
三至四年的平台週期，AI 伺服器迭代速度明顯加快，GPU、ASIC 與交換器間
的資料傳輸需求快速增加，推動網路架構由 400G、800G 向 1.6T、3.2T 演
進。隨訊號速率提升，傳輸損耗與訊號完整性要求同步提高，板材須持續降低
介電常數與介電損耗，帶動 CCL 由 M7、M8 向 M9 以上升級；玻纖布作為影
響板材電性的重要材料，亦由傳統 E-glass 逐步升級至一代、二代低介電
布，並進一步朝石英布與第三代低介電材料發展。 
就目前材料演進路徑而言，400G 交換器主要採用 M7 板材搭配一代低介電
布，800G 升級至 M8 後，一代與二代低介電布開始並用；進入 1.6T 世代
後，二代低介電布的重要性進一步提升，部分高階規格則開始評估導入石英
布。展望 2028 年後，隨 3.2T 逐步導入，材料規格預期將進一步朝升級版石
英布或第三代低介電布發展，高階玻纖布的滲透率持續提升。 
研究部認為，規格升級將成為本輪玻纖布產業成長的核心驅動力。隨 AI 運算
與高速傳輸規格持續演進，材料需求將由傳統 E-glass 加速轉向低介電、低
膨脹等高階產品。在終端平台持續更替的背景下，玻纖布須隨板材世代同步升
級，而每一次規格提升亦伴隨 ASP 與單位產值增加，使產業成長不僅來自需
求量擴張，更來自產品組合持續高階化。

表二、CCL 等級與對應玻纖布

資料來源：統一投顧整理

AI 運算密度與傳輸速率持續提
升，帶動 PCB 材料由 E-glass 
向低介電、低膨脹等高階玻纖布
升級，推升高階產品滲透率、ASP 
與單位產值，成為產業成長的核
心驅動力。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

專利與認證構成高階玻纖布的實質進入障礙 
高階玻纖布的核心技術門檻在玻纖紗配方，低介電與低膨脹材料的關鍵專利亦
主要集中於紗端，後進者若要切入，須重新設計配方以迴避既有專利，良率與
量產時程因而受限。另一方面，CCL 廠需將特定玻纖布與樹脂、銅箔共同調
校，一旦納入合格供應商名單，更換材料通常須重新開立料號並進行完整驗
證，使認證週期可長達數年。富喬憑藉紗、布垂直整合，可直接由紗端調整配
方與製程，並已完成多地高階低介電玻纖布專利布局，低膨脹布亦已突破既有
專利限制並通過終端認證。研究部認為專利保護與長認證週期共同構成高階玻
纖布的實質進入障礙，亦使中國廠商產能雖快速擴張，短期內能實際轉化為高
階產品供給的比例仍有限。

供需失衡與料源綁定 
玻纖布供需失衡自 2026 年起由通用布延伸至高階布，主要瓶頸集中於織機、
良率與認證。玻纖布織機由豐田獨家供應，新機交期已排至 2031 年，且超薄
與特殊布須降速生產，使名目機台數無法等比轉化為實際產出；另一方面，高
階布認證週期普遍超過兩年，也使既有產能難以在短期內快速切換料號。 
E 布短缺主要來自龍頭廠商退出低階產能。日東紡、旭化成陸續停產 E-glass 
布後，供給收縮帶動各類 E 布報價自 2025 年底至 2026 年普遍上漲逾一倍
以上。惟中國新增產能自 2027 年陸續開出，加上消費性需求仍偏弱，研究部
預期 E 布供需將於 2027 年逐步緩解，報價漲幅趨緩。 
一代低介電布供需相對平衡，惟隨二代布需求放大，具備雙線能力的廠商傾向
將織機轉向二代布。二代低介電布則為目前供給最為吃緊的品項，主要對應 
M8 以上板材。除織機供給受限外，良率更是限制有效產出的關鍵，新進者量
產良率多不足五成，使名目產能與實際出貨量存在明顯落差，也支撐二代布報
價維持在一代布的約 2 至 3 倍。 
低膨脹布的供給缺口則可能延續更久。隨先進封裝尺寸放大與層數增加，而供
應仍高度集中於日東紡，新產能最快自 2H27 起陸續開出。石英布則面臨不同
瓶頸，問題不在供給，而在加工良率與導入進度；由於鑽孔與除膠渣難度較
高，部分原規劃採用石英布的設計轉回二代低介電布，反而延長二代布的產品
週期。 
供給吃緊亦同步改變材料認證與採購模式。過去玻纖布多由 CCL 廠與板廠選
料，現階段雲端業者與系統廠已逐步直接認證布廠並指定料源，部分客戶另要
求非中國產能，CCL 廠亦透過長約提前鎖定高階布供給。高階玻纖布的競爭因
而由單純產能擴張，轉向認證、良率與料源掌握能力的競爭。

高階玻纖布具備配方專利與長認
證週期雙重門檻，限制新進者有
效產能釋放，也使富喬憑藉紗布
垂直整合與既有認證基礎建立明
顯競爭優勢。

玻纖布供給受限於織機、良率與
認證門檻，高階產品短缺延續並
推動客戶直接綁定料源，產業競
爭 重 心 由 產 能 擴 張 轉 向 認 證 能
力、量產良率與供應穩定性。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖三、二代低介電布供需缺口

資料來源：統一投顧預估

產能配置與產品組合 
富喬目前共有約 920 台織布機，其中台灣 490 台、東莞 430 台。受限於織
布機交期，富喬 2027 年以前的主要成長動能，是將既有機台由 E 布、一代
低介電布持續轉向單位產值與毛利率更高的二代低介電布，透過產品組合升級
放大既有產能效益。 
台灣廠部分機台維持生產 E 布，供應台系 CCL 廠與低軌衛星應用，其餘產能
則持續轉向二代低介電布，低膨脹布亦預計自 2027 年起逐步導入。海外方
面，東莞現有 430 台於 2026 年仍以 E 布為主，自 1Q27 起採台灣供紗、當
地織布模式分批轉作二代低介電布。泰國一期則規劃新增 500 台織機，預計
自 2H27 起分批到位，主要生產低介電布並承接客戶對非中國產能的需求。 
富喬能快速進行跨廠區與跨產品配置，關鍵在於紗、布垂直整合。公司自有電
子級玻纖紗可隨布種升級同步調整紗的配方與規格，降低對外部紗源的依賴。
隨台灣產能持續高階化、東莞轉產二代布及泰國新產能加入，研究部預估二代
低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 
51%，同期毛利率亦由 27.6% 升至 44.7% 與 54.8%。

富喬以既有機台轉產高毛利二代
低介電布，並透過東莞轉線與泰
國新產能擴充高階產品供給，帶
動二代布營收占比與整體毛利率
持續提升。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 7

圖四、富喬產品組合演變（營收占比）

資料來源：統一投顧預估

營運概況 
1. 2H26 營運將改寫新高 
富喬 7、8 月營收分別為 8.54 億元（+24.0% MoM、+68.1% YoY）與 9.12 億
元（+6.8% MoM、+79.2% YoY） ，連續改寫單月新高。隨新一代 AI 伺服器與 
1.6T 交換器平台陸續放量，二代低介電布需求同步攀升，加以 6 月起調漲的
報價自 3Q26 全季認列，研究部預估 3Q26 營收 27.66 億元（+29.8% QoQ、
+80.4% YoY） ，改寫單季歷史新高。毛利率升至 48.8%（+8.8ppt QoQ、
+21.9ppt YoY） ，除產品組合與報價效益外，二代布量產良率亦已逐步改善。
營業利益 10.39 億元（+89.8% QoQ、+301.5% YoY） ，營益率 37.6%；稅後淨
利 8.58 億元（+1.8% QoQ、+203.2% YoY），EPS 1.40 元，EPS 較 2Q26 小幅
下滑係因盈餘轉增資後股數增加。展望 4Q26，隨著二代布出貨比重持續提升
以及良率續揚貢獻，研究部預估營收 30.51 億元（+10.3% QoQ、+86.4% 
YoY） 、毛利率 49.9%、EPS 1.62 元。

2. 2027 年成長動能轉向產品組合升級，EPS 估 9.30 元 
2027 年富喬獲利成長動能將由價格上漲轉向產品組合升級與新增產能貢獻。
台灣廠持續將既有機台由一代轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣供
紗、當地織布模式，分批將 E 布產能轉作二代布；泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐
步投產，主要承接客戶對非中國高階產能的需求。隨三地產能同步往高階產品
配置，研究部預估二代低介電布營收占比將由 2026 年的 27% 提升至 2027 
年的 51%，成為獲利成長的主要來源。研究部預估 2027 年營收 149.82 億元
（+52.4% YoY） ，並隨泰國產能開出及二代布占比提升呈逐季成長；毛利率升
至 54.8%，營益率達 45.7%，帶動稅後淨利 57.18 億元（+81.4% YoY），EPS 
9.30 元。

2H26 受惠二代低介電布需求放
量 、 漲價效益與良率改善 ， 富喬營
收與毛利率同步攀升，研究部預
期 3Q26、4Q26 營運將連續改寫
新高。

2027 年富喬成長動能將由漲價
轉 向 產 品 組 合 升 級 與 新 產 能 貢
獻 ， 隨台灣 、 東莞及泰國產能加速
轉向二代低介電布，研究部預估
二代布營收占比升至 51%，帶動 
EPS 成長至 9.30 元。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 8

投資建議 
研究部初次給予富喬買進投資評等，主要考量：(1) 石英布受鑽孔損耗與加
工良率限制 ， 導入時程遞延 ， 終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布搭配高階樹脂的
架構，延長二代布產品週期，帶動富喬二代低介電布營收占比由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%；(2) 玻纖布織布機由豐田獨
家供應，目前新機交期已排至 2031 年，意味 2027 年以前產業新增供給主
要仰賴既有機台重新配置 ， 富喬可在無須大幅增加資本支出的情況下 ， 持續將
既有產能由 E 布轉向一代及二代低介電布，研究部推算二代布毛利率較一代
布高逾 10 個百分點，產品組合優化將推升毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%，提
升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 2027 年的 54.8%；(3) 東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣
供紗、當地織布模式，分批將既有產能轉作二代低介電布，泰國一期則規劃  
500 台織機，自 2H27 起逐步開出，同樣以低介電布為主要產品，並承接客
戶對非中國產能的需求 。綜上所述 ， 研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 
分別為 5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後純益分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富
喬為日系龍頭以外少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方 能力的供應商，以  
2027 年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買
進投資評等。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

營收走勢圖

電子–通信網路  
全新(2455.TT) 
產能不是問題，基板供應改善推升營收獲利成長

研究部初次給予全新買進建議，主因為：(1)InP 基板供應改善帶
動光電子快速放量，且高毛利光電子占比由 1H26 約 26%提升至 7 月
40%、8 月 46.1%，光電子超越微電子的黃金交叉將近；(2)產品組合轉
佳 、 較高毛利的客供料占比逐步提升及價格調整效益帶動獲利改善 ， 管
理層預期 3Q26 毛利率可回升至 40%以上；(3)展望 2027 年，美系客戶
200G PD 預計 1Q27 開始明顯放量，CW Laser 及 EML 發射端則接續放
量，管理層預期2027 年發射端營收有望超越接收端。綜上所述，研究
部預估全新2026/2027 營收為49.02/78.77 億元 ， 分別年增45%/60.7%，
EPS 分別為 6.08/11.63 元，評價方面，考量目前上游磊晶需求持續強
勁，且基板供應逐步改善有望帶動營收獲利成長，因此給予60 倍本益
比，目標價 700 元(60PER*2027EPS)。

1. 3Q26 基板供貨穩定帶動高毛利光電子營收佔比提升 
研究部預估3Q26 營收將季增 44.2%達13.92 億元，EPS 為1.69 元，
主係:(1)基板供應穩定加上客戶自行攜帶基板數量增加；(2)高毛
利光電子營收佔比持續提升 。 預估毛利率達42.3%， 季增3.7ppts，
短期營運動能持續向上。

2. MOCVD 產能持續擴充，產能不成問題，2027 年光電子成長可期 
7 月整體MOCVD 稼動率僅約 50%，2027 年GaAs 及InP 共用的MOCVD
機台數新增至 66 台，後續有望隨 InP 需求，調整成 InP 產能使
用，滿足光電子產品後續需求。展望 2027 年，接收端 200G PD 於
1Q27 開始放量，發射端 CW Laser 及 EML 於 1H27 開始量產，且發
射端有望超越接收端，成為 2027 年光電子營收成長動能。

獲利及評價資訊

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
全新光電（2455）成立於1996 年，為全球主要Ⅲ-V 族化合物半導體專業磊
晶廠，以MOCVD（金屬有機化學氣相沉積）為核心技術，主要提供客戶磊晶
晶圓 ， 產品可分為微電子及光電子兩大領域 。 微電子產品以GaAs HBT、PHEMT
等磊晶片為主，主要應用於手機、Wi-Fi、基地台等射頻功率放大器 （PA），
目前為全球最大 GaAs PA 磊晶供應商；光電子產品則涵蓋 InGaAs PD/APD、
FP/DFB Laser 及 VCSEL 等磊晶片，應用於光通訊、資料中心、LiDAR 及感測
等市場。2Q26 產品組合為微電子 73%、光電子 27%。

圖一、全新 2Q26 營收佔比

資料來源：全新，統一投顧整理

產業概況 
(1) 手機進入成長高原期，WiFi7/8 與新興終端擴大GaAs 應用場景 
GaAs憑藉高頻、高線性度及高功率效率等特性，長期為無線通訊RF前端的
重要材料，主要應用於PA、LNA及部分Switch，其中HBT仍為手機與WiFi PA
的主流技術。過去GaAs需求主要隨智慧型手機通訊規格升級而成長，隨4G
進入5G後，新增頻段使PA數量及RF前端複雜度同步提升，5G手機所需GaAs
磊晶面積約較4G增加20%～30%，帶動單機GaAs價值持續提高。

然而，隨全球智慧型手機市場逐步成熟，未來GaAs需求成長動能將由過去
依賴手機出貨量增加 ， 轉向無線通訊規格升級與連網終端數量擴張 。 其中 ，
WiFi 7為目前最明確的新增需求來源，除手機、PC及Router持續升級外，
應用範圍亦逐步延伸至AI眼鏡 、 無人機 、 機器人 、 智慧家庭及其他IoT設備 。
隨終端裝置導入更高速、更低延遲的無線連接規格，對PA輸出功率、線性
度與效率的要求同步提升，使 GaAs需求不再僅與智慧型手機銷售高度連
動，而是逐步擴展至更廣泛的連網設備市場。

全新為全球 最大砷化鎵磊晶廠 ，
近期受光通訊需求增加，光電子
產品占比逐步提升。

手機終端需求成長放緩，然規格
升級及終端應用增加，帶動 GaAs
需求提升。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 3

下一世代WiFi 8則有望延續此一規格升級趨勢。相較過去WiFi世代主要追
求傳輸速度提升，WiFi 8進一步強調低延遲、穩定連線、長距離覆蓋與更
高上行流量(uplink)效能，應用場景也更貼近AI Edge Device、機器人及
即時互聯設備。整體而言，GaAs微電子產業的成長邏輯由手機出貨成長轉
向單機RF價值提升及連網裝置數量增加。

(2)  AI光互連升級推升InP需求，成為Ⅲ-V族主要成長動能 
生成式AI帶動CSP持續擴大資料中心投資，隨GPU與ASIC叢集規模增加，光
模組規格由400G快速升級至800G、1.6T，並朝3.2T發展，使高速PD、EML及
CW Laser 需 求 同 步 提 升 。LightCounting 於2026/7 最 新 預 估 ，2026 年
Ethernet光收發模組銷售額將年增73%，並預估2031年市場規模達800億美
元，反映AI高速光互連需求仍處於快速成長階段。

隨單通道速率由100G提升至200G， 接收端PD對頻寬要求提高 ， 發射端Laser
亦朝更高功率與高速率升級，使InP成為高速光通訊的關鍵材料。另一方
面，SiPh滲透率提升並不會取代InP， 因矽本身無法有效發光 ， 仍須搭配InP 
CW Laser 作為光源；後續 NPO/CPO 導 入 亦 將 延 續 外置 光 源 需 求 。同時
LightCounting指出在近期需求再度上修後，原先預期緩解的InP Laser短
缺可能延續至2027年中，顯示InP需求增速仍高於供給擴張。

(3) 上游原料高度集中中國，地緣政治加劇Ⅲ-V族供給限制 
Ⅲ-V族上游原料供給高度集中於中國，其中全球約98%的Gallium產量來自
中國 ，InP所需Indium亦約70%由中國供應 ， 使GaAs與InP供應鏈對中國政策
高度敏感 。 隨中美科技競爭升溫 ， 中國自2023年起對Gallium及相關化合物
實施出口許可，2025年再將InP基板納入出口管制，目前GaAs、Ge及InP基
板出口均須取得中國商務部許可，部分產品供貨時程已受到審批進度影
響。因此，原料供應高度集中與出口管制已成為影響Ⅲ-V族材料供應穩定
性及出貨時程的重要變數。

營運回顧及展望分析 
1. 2Q26 本業穩健改善，匯損低基期推升獲利大幅成長 
全新2Q26營收達9.65億元(+0.6%QoQ，+35.2%YoY)， 創單季歷史新高 ， 主要
受惠AI資料中心相關光電子需求持續成長，以及 Wi-Fi等微電子需求維持
穩健。毛利率為38.61%(+0.5ppts QoQ，+4.2ppts YoY)，主要受惠產品漲
價轉嫁基板及原物料成本，加上高毛利光電子產品占比提升。營業費用達
1.49億元(+7.3%QoQ，+20.6%YoY)、業外利益達0.13億元 ， 較去年同期業外
損失0.98億元大幅改善(2Q25受到新台幣急升造成匯兌損失)，使2Q26稅後
淨 利 達2.14 億元(+9.5%QoQ ，+488.3%YoY) ，EPS 為1.15 元(+8.5%QoQ ，
+475%YoY)。2Q26產品組合方面：微電子73%、光電子27%；相較1Q26微電子
75%、光電子25%，光電子營收占比持續提升。

光互連高速成長，接收發射端皆
需要 InP 關鍵材料。

鎵及銦原物料供應主要來自中國，
地緣政治成為供貨穩定的變數。

受惠於光電子需求持續成長且佔
比提升，營收創歷史新高且毛利
率季增年增。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

2. 基板到位推升光電子放量，3Q26 營收獲利可望再創高 
全新 7 月 自 結 ， 營 收 達4.22 億元 (+45.11%YoY) 、 稅 後 淨 利1.15 億元
(+236.97%YoY)、EPS為0.62元 。 研究部以過往匯兌損益以及費用率去預估 ，
毛利率約為42%， 優於2Q26的38.6%， 受惠高毛利光電子產品比重提升 。8月
營收達4.71億元(+11.6%MoM，+71.2%YoY)， 累計3Q26達成率為67%， 符合研
究 部 預 期 。 產 品 組 合 方 面 ， 微 電 子 53.9%(-6ppts MoM) ，光電子
46.1%(+6ppts MoM)，光電子佔比提升受惠InP基板出口許可陸續取得、基
板供應改善 ， 以及客戶自帶基板增加 ； 然微電子進入3Q傳統手機新品旺季 ，
亦使營收穩健成長。研究部預期在光通訊需求高速成長下，預期4Q26有機
會超越微電子產品形成交叉，進而帶動整體毛利率。研究部預估3Q26營收
為13.92億元，EPS為1.69元。

圖二、全新產品組合佔比

資料來源：全新，統一投顧整理

3. 200G PD 1Q27 放量，CW Laser、EML 接續推升發射端成長  
接收端方面，目前新增PD專案以200G為主，美系大客戶已進入量產。目前
200G PD 占比仍低，但美系客戶客供料已陸續進廠，預計1Q27開始明顯放
量，屆時200G PD有機會占整體PD營收50%以上。200G PD技術門檻及ASP均
明顯優於100G，因此除帶動營收成長外，產品組合亦將持續往高階移動。
發射端方面，CW Laser目前已進入量產並開始出貨，2026年仍以小量出貨
為主 ， 預計2027年隨既有客戶需求提升進一步放量 。 此外 ， 目前已有陸系 、
台系及日系等多家客戶合作 ， 公司亦正與美系大客戶洽談新案 ；EML目前在
客戶驗證階段，預計於1H27開始放量，將成為發射端新增營收來源。管理
層預期2027年隨1.6T需求成長、CW Laser 及EML出貨規模擴大，以及發射
端ASP相較接收端1.5倍以上，發射端營收有望超越接收端，成為光電子下
一階段主要成長來源。

7 月及 8 月高毛利光電子營收佔
比提升，3Q26 獲利有望創高。

2027 年發射端營收佔比有望超越
接收端，成為光電子產品下一階
段成長動能。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

4. 既有產能彈性轉向 InP，新增機台支撐 2027 光電子成長 
全新目前共有67台MOCVD，其中64台可於GaAs及InP間彈性共用、3台為GaN
專用設備 。7月營收雖創歷史新高 ， 但產能利用率仍僅50%以上，顯示MOCVD
設備本身具有餘裕， 現階段限制光電子出貨的主要因素仍為 InP基板供應
限制。 由於GaAs與InP共用機台轉換僅需3–5天 ， 若2027年基板供應持續改
善，公司可依訂單需求及產品毛利率，逐步將部分低毛利微電子產能轉向
PD、CW Laser、EML等高毛利InP光電子產品。近期，公司因應需求再新增
2台MOCVD供InP產能使用 ， 預計3Q27到廠 ， 屆時總機台數將由67台提升至69
台。

需求端方面，Lumentum持續擴充產能，但供給仍然落後客戶需求，供需缺
口由原先的25-30%擴大至30%以上。因此研究部認為，在磷化銦基板供應逐
步改善以及供需吃緊下，全新有望將既有GaAs/InP共用機台產能轉換，逐
步轉向高單價、高毛利光電子產品。綜上所述，研究部保守假設2027年部
分GaAs產能轉為InP使用 ， 搭配新增2台InP設備貢獻 ， 預估2027年光電子營
收年增138%。

投資建議 
產能不是問題，基板供應改善推升營收獲利成長

研究部初次給予全新買進建議 ， 主因為 ：(1)InP 基板供應改善帶動光電子
快速放量 ， 且高毛利光電子占比由1H26 約26%提升至7 月40%、8 月46.1%，
光電子超越微電子的黃金交叉將近 ；(2)產品組合轉佳 、較高毛利的客供料
占比逐步提升及價格調整效益帶動獲利改善，管理層預期 3Q26 毛利率可
回升至 40%以上；(3)展望 2027 年，美系客戶200G PD 預計 1Q27 開始明顯
放量，CW Laser 及 EML 發射端則接續放量，管理層預期 2027 年發射端營
收 有 望 超 越 接 收 端 。 綜 上 所 述 ， 研 究 部 預 估 全 新2026/2027 營收為
49.02/78.77 億元，分別年增 45%/60.7%，EPS 分別為 6.08/11.63 元，評
價方面，考量目前上游磊晶需求持續強勁，且基板供應逐步改善有望帶動
營收獲利成長，因此給予 60 倍本益比，目標價 700 元(60PER*2027EPS)。

2027 年MOCVD 總台數共 69 台，66
台為 GaAs 及 InP 共用 ， 後續可依
需求轉換成 InP 產能。

提 供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見，不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其
他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證

Co-Tech Development Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Southeast Asia expansion points
to longer-term capacity upside
What’s new? Co-Tech’s board approved the establishment of a subsidiary in
Southeast Asia.
Our view: We expect the facility to primarily produce RG-series (advanced RTF)
copper foil, mainly used in general servers, switches or LEO satellites. Volume
production could begin in 2H28, with monthly capacity exceeding 500 tons. We
currently model Co-Tech’s RG-series capacity at 1,000 tons by end-2026 and 1,200

tons by end-2027. The Southeast Asia expansion therefore implies upside to our
current capacity assumptions, assuming all else remains unchanged, with
contribution likely starting in 2H28. We also believe adding RG-series capacity in
Southeast Asia could free up existing Taiwan capacity for higher-end HVLP4+ copper
foil production. Stay OW.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 072: 260915_ms_MLCC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260915_ms_MLCC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-15
Pages: 43
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

M
Japan Insight
2
Investment Perspective
MLCC is early cycle in AI server & rack penetration
There is immense change underway for the MLCC industry with an investment narrative
that is changing as AI chips/server demand is increasingly driving MLCC scarcity.  AI is
shifting from semiconductors to MLCCs, creating a supply/demand mismatch in high-end
components. The high-end AI MLCC market is effectively a Japanese-Korean duopoly with
Murata and SEMCO together hold around 85% share. High-capacitance, low-ESL parts for

AI accelerators require hundreds to over a thousand dielectric layers with near-zero
defects and face strict customer qualification — the barrier preventing a repeat of 2017-
18, when shortages invited entry and the cycle self-corrected. AI platforms now compete
directly with consumer, automotive, and industrial applications for advanced materials,
heightening supply risk for MLCC traditional customers. We are still in the early innings,
and supply concerns are extending beyond near-term shortages to longer-term supply

security. As a result, we are seeing an increasing number of long-term supply agreements
being signed between suppliers and customers.
Exhibit 1: Sequencing the technology cycle – still early cycle for MLCC
Source:  Morgan Stanley Research estimates
Turning point in MLCC pricing
While Murata Mfg.’s CFO and Executive Deputy President Masanori Minamide stated in a
Sep 14 Nikkei interview that rising production loads for high-end products are making it

increasingly difficult to fully supply low-end products, and that the priority of defending
low-end market share has effectively declined, we believe the company’s fundamental
policy of not changing prices based solely on short-term supply-demand conditions
remains intact. That said, in our view, Murata has been gradually shifting its strategy
toward focusing more on its areas of strength and adopting pricing that better reflects the
need to cover large-scale capital investments, particularly in those core segments where it

enjoys a competitive advantage. We believe that Murata’s reluctance to enter into L TAs for
AI/data-center use value added MLCCs reflects its view that locking in prices today may
not be optimal, given the potential for MLCC pricing to rise in the future.
We expect sales growth to continue for small-sized, high-
capacity, high-value-added MLCCs for AI servers/DCs

### Page 3

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 3
MLCCs are a key business, accounting for 50% of sales at Murata, 46% at SEMCO, 19% at
Yageo, and 70% at Taiyo Yuden in 2025. We expect global MLCC shipment value to
expand at a CAGR of 20.3%, from $18.51bn in 2025 to $44.45bn in 2031. We believe
growth is set to accelerate from the 5.0% CAGR recorded in global MLCC shipment value
over the 13 years of 2012-25 because we expect continued growth in sales of small, high-

capacity, high-value-added MLCCs for AI servers/datacenters (DCs) from 2026. For AI
servers/DCs alone, we forecast MLCC shipment value to reach $23.33bn by 2031 (vs. our
previous estimate of $10.80bn), implying a 57.1% CAGR (vs. 38.1%).
Howard Kao (our Greater China Technology Hardware analyst) expects MLCC demand
volume for VR200 NVL72 racks to increase about 80% versus GB300 NVL72 racks, and
forecasts the number of MLCCs mounted in each Rubin NVL72 rack will jump to roughly

570,000 units from around 320,000 for GB300 NVL72 racks, with MLCC dollar content
per rack estimated to increase by 166%.　We expect the rapid expansion of the high-value-
added MLCC market for AI servers/DCs to be especially positive for  earnings at Murata (#1
market share in MLCCs for 2025 at 41%) and SEMCO (#2 at 23%).

Key roles of MLCCs in AI/DC
MLCCs play a role in locally stabilizing power supply (instantaneous current supply plus

noise removal) near semiconductors, such as GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. Since GPUs, CPUs,
and FPGAs repeatedly switch on and off on a nanosecond timescale, they demand large
currents instantaneously, and MLCCs meet this instantaneous current demand by
immediately discharging their stored charge. As semiconductor miniaturization advances,
circuit operating speed increases and drive voltage falls. As voltage decreases, even small
voltage fluctuations risk causing semiconductor malfunction, so even more stable power

supply voltage is required than before. Demand for capacitor capacitance is also increasing
in line with AI servers' rising computing power and power consumption, and because
board area and mountable space are limited, we expect demand for smaller, higher-
capacity MLCCs to persist.
Sharp dips vs. share price peaks provide re-entry opportunity
Year-to-date to Sep 11, share prices of the four companies had risen 127% for Murata,
449% for SEMCO, 141% for Yageo, and 160% for Taiyo Yuden. However, from their

respective June-July peaks, these have fallen 40% for Murata, 38% for SEMCO, 52% for

### Page 4

M
Japan Insight
4
Yageo, and 59% for Taiyo Yuden. Considering the strong likelihood that high-value-added
products for AI servers/DCs will continue to drive growth in MLCC demand not only in
2026 but also from 2027 onward, and that each company's earnings will stay on an
expansionary trend, we believe the conditions are now in place to consider re-entering
these stocks.

Earnings and share price risks
We expect capital expenditure on AI/DC to keep expanding and generational transitions to

### Page 6

M
Japan Insight
6
continue in GPUs, sustaining growth in demand for small, high-capacity, high-value-added
MLCCs. Risks to the continuity of AI/DC capex expansion include: (1) rising risk from the
growing practice of hyperscalers and major semiconductor companies charging usage fees
in exchange for equity investments in AI development companies, conditional on the use
of their own cloud services or semiconductors; and (2) risk that AI/DC capex growth slows

due to bottlenecks, such as power infrastructure constraints. If the pace of AI/DC capex
growth slows, we cannot rule out that MLCC demand falls short of our base case outlook
for demand. Also, as shown in Exhibits Exhibit 8 Exhibit 8  and Exhibit 9 , during the IT
bubble around 2000, there was a strong correlation between the YoY change in Murata's
and Taiyo Yuden's monthly order intake and their market cap. Since order intake has risen
sharply since Jul-Sep 2025, we cannot ignore the possibility that YoY growth in order

intake will slow. On the other hand, MLCC demand continues to expand for high-value-
added products for AI/DC, and we expect the major MLCC makers' sales to grow along
with an improving product mix.

High-value-added products for AI servers will likely drive
MLCC market growth
We expect global MLCC shipment value to increase at 20.3%
CAGR  through 2031
As shown in Exhibit 10 ,  we estimate that global MLCC shipment value fell for two
consecutive years, from $17.47bn in 2021 (up 26.0% YoY) to $14.32bn in 2022 (down
18.1%) and $12.64bn in 2023 (down 11.7%), before rising back up to $13.30bn in 2024 (up
5.2%) and $14.67bn in 2025 (up 10.3%). We increase our forecasts for shipment value from

$18.48bn to $18.51bn for 2026 (up 26.2% YoY), from $22.16bn to $24.52bn for 2027 (up
32.4%), and from $25.73bn to $28.60bn for 2028 (up 16.7%), and we expect the market to
reach $44.45bn in 2031, growing at a CAGR of 20.3% over the six-year period.

AI/DC MLCC market to continue growing at a rapid pace
We expect growth in demand to accelerate for high-value-added products for AI/DC from

the second half of 2026. Howard Kao  expects MLCC demand volume for VR200 NVL72
racks to increase about 80% versus GB300 NVL72 racks, and forecasts the number of
MLCCs mounted in each Rubin NVL72 rack will rise sharply to roughly 570,000 units from
around 320,000 units for GB300 NVL72 racks (Exhibit 11 ). This increase stems from a
rise in the number of MLCCs mounted on both compute boards and switch boards, as well
as an increase in PCB content across the overall system, including modules such as CX9

Orchid. Howard Kao estimates this increase in MLCC mounting volume will lift the dollar-
based MLCC content per rack by 166% ( Exhibit 12 ).

Role of MLCCs in AI servers
Capacitors, including multi-layer ceramic capacitors (MLCCs), function as a “dam” that
temporarily stores electricity, and are an essential electronic component for noise removal

and voltage stabilization in virtually all electronic circuits, including those used in
computers, smartphones, and automobiles. MLCCs play a role in locally stabilizing power
supply (instantaneous current supply plus noise removal) near semiconductors such as
GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. Since GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs repeatedly switch on and off on a
nanosecond timescale, they demand large currents instantaneously, and MLCCs meet this
instantaneous current demand by immediately discharging their stored charge. At the

same time, MLCCs bypass only the AC component (noise and fluctuating current), which
also serves to prevent noise from entering GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. MLCCs feature low
ESR (low resistance) and low ESL (low inductance), allowing them to track even

### Page 10

M
Japan Insight
10
nanosecond-level current fluctuations in semiconductors such as GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. 
MLCCs are often placed around components such as GPUs and ASICs that operate at low 
voltages of around 1.0V. As semiconductor miniaturization advances, circuit operating 
speed increases and drive voltage falls. As voltage decreases, even small voltage 
fluctuations risk causing semiconductor malfunction, requiring an even more stable power 
supply voltage than before. Demand for capacitor capacitance will also rise along with AI 
servers' improving computing power and increasing power consumption, and because 
board area and mountable space are limited, demand for smaller, higher-capacity MLCCs 
will likely continue. 
MLCC miniaturization and higher capacity to accelerate with 
VR200 NVL72
We assume that the space available for mounting MLCCs on VR200 NVL72 will not 
change significantly from the GB300 NVL72 rack, and that the transition from GB300 to 
VR200 will require installing higher-capacity MLCCs within the limited space of 
semiconductor ABF package substrates and AI accelerator boards, accelerating growth in 
demand for small-sized, high-capacity MLCCs. Murata has consistently been a leader in 
MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases, beginning mass production of 1005-size 1 F 
MLCCs in 2000 and 0402-size 1 F and 1005-size 22 F MLCCs in 2020. We expect 
demand for 1608-size 100 F, 1005-size 47 F, and 0603-size 10 F MLCCs to increase 
substantially with VR200 NVL72. We estimate that 1608-size 100 F MLCCs command a 
unit price 5-10x higher than 2012-size 100 F or 1608-size 47 F MLCCs, and we expect 
the shift to smaller, higher-capacity MLCCs accompanying the transition to VR200 NVL72 
to drive a substantial rise in ASP.

High-capacitance MLCCs (47µF+) are likely to capture a disproportionate share of this 
incremental opportunity: Our analysis suggests that 47µF+ MLCCs could account for 
approximately 50-60% of the incremental Cloud AI MLCC TAM in CY27, representing an 
additional ~US$2B market opportunity alone. This reflects both the increasing use of high-

### Page 11

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 11
capacitance devices within AI accelerator systems and a growing mix shift toward higher-
value MLCC content.
From a volume perspective, we estimate Cloud AI demand for 47µF+ MLCCs could 
increase from approximately 4 billion units in CY25 to 40B+ units by CY27: Such a 
rapid increase would represent a meaningful step-up in industry demand and could require 
substantial capacity reallocation or expansion among leading suppliers.
Based on our industry discussions, the market for high-capacitance MLCCs remains 
concentrated among a limited number of suppliers, primarily Japanese and Korean 
manufacturers, including Murata, Taiyo Yuden, and Samsung Electro-Mechanics (SEMCO). 
As demand from AI infrastructure accelerates, we believe capacity utilization for these 
products could tighten, raising the possibility of capacity crowding-out effects across 
other end markets and applications.

Materials development and production technology behind
MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases
In MLCCs, smaller sizes and higher capacities have mainly been achieved by thinning the
dielectric and electrode layers (layer thinning) and increasing the number of layers
stacked (multilayering). For high-capacity products for AI/DC, in addition to the layer
thinning and multilayering described above, efforts are accelerating to further miniaturize

and increase capacity by expanding the area of the dielectric and internal electrodes per
dielectric sheet. We believe MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases depend on: (1)
developing superior dielectric and electrode materials; (2) manufacturing highly uniform,
highly reliable slurry; (3) technology for precisely　stacking dielectric sheets; and (4)
technology for firing in a controlled atmosphere without oxidation. For high-capacity
products for AI/DC, we see the key factors as (1) materials development that increases the

crystallinity of dielectric materials while making them finer, in order to thin each dielectric
layer as much as possible (layer thinning), together with (2) manufacturing technology to
precisely stack the dielectric sheets.

Dielectric material manufacturing process
Dielectric materials are manufactured by adding a binder, solvent, and additives to barium
titanate (BaTiO3), which is produced from barium carbonate (B aCO3) and titanium oxide

(TiO2). Methods for manufacturing barium titanate are broadly divided into the solid-
phase method and the liquid-phase method. In the solid-phase method, barium carbonate
and titanium oxide are mixed and fired at high temperature, producing barium titanate
through a chemical reaction at the firing stage. In the liquid-phase method, barium
carbonate and titanium oxide are dissolved in a solution, reacted at the solution stage, and
fired at a relatively low temperature to produce the compound. Manufacturing methods

used in mass production via the liquid-phase method include the oxalate method and the
hydrothermal synthesis method. Barium titanate with small and uniform particle size is
key to smaller and higher-capacity MLCCs. In 2023, Murata established MF Materials, a
joint venture with Ishihara Sangyo and Ishihara Sangyo's wholly owned subsidiary Fuji
Titanium (with equity stakes of 20% for Murata, 10% for Ishihara Sangyo, and 70% for Fuji
Titanium). MF Materials produces barium titanate via the oxalate process and supplies it

to Murata Manufacturing.　MF Materials is constructing a new building adjacent to its head
office plant in Nobeoka, Miyazaki Prefecture, with operations scheduled to commence in
2027. TDK established a joint venture with Nippon Chemical Industrial in April 2026 called
TDK-NCI Advanced Materials (with equity stakes of 51% for TDK and 49% for Nippon
Chemical Industrial), with plans to accelerate development of the oxalate method barium
titanate.

Murata and SEMCO to benefit the most from growing
demand for high-value-added products for AI servers
We expect Murata and SEMCO, which supply small, high-capacity products stably and in
large volumes, to capture the greatest benefits from growing demand for small-sized,
high-capacity MLCCs used in AI servers and data centers. Murata has explained that AI/DC

MLCC sales accounted for 10-15% of its MLCC sales in F3/26 and are forecast to rise 100-
105% YoY in F3/27, accounting for 20-25% of MLCC sales. We estimate Murata's AI/DC
MLCC sales to rise roughly 120% YoY in F3/27 and about 100% YoY in F3/28, with AI/DC
MLCCs underpinning growth in the company's overall MLCC sales. Murata has stated that
it forecasts a 100-105% increase in AI/DC MLCC sales in F3/27, reflecting a roughly 40%
increase in volume and a roughly 50% rise in ASP, but we expect the increase in ASP to

exceed its forecast.
2025 MLCC market share was Murata 40.8%, Taiyo Yuden
11.3%
We estimate that global MLCC shipment value totaled $14.67bn in 2025 (up 10.3% YoY),
with shares of 40.8% for Murata, 22.5% for SEMCO, 11.3% for Taiyo Yuden, 6.9% for TDK,
and 5.4% for Yageo, and a combined 13.0% for all companies outside these top five. We
expect demand for high-value-added products for AI/DC to keep expanding from 2027,
with Murata and SEMCO continuing to gain market share.

What are the risks to Murata?
Risks for Murata include: (1) losing MLCC market share by failing to adequately supply
MLCCs for AI servers/DCs amid rapidly expanding demand. (2) Murata's products,
including MLCCs, are used not only in AI servers/DCs but across virtually all electronic
equipment, including automobiles, smartphones, and industrial machinery, making the
company sensitive to macroeconomic conditions and inventory cycles. (3) The company

has explained that a ¥1/$ appreciation of the yen would reduce operating profit by ¥4.5bn
per year, and the yen could appreciate beyond our assumed rate of ¥155/$, among other
factors.

Murata Manufacturing earnings forecasts
We expect Murata at 2Q results (end-Oct) to hike F3/27
guidance; continued earnings growth from F3/28
F3/27-28 and F3/27 2Q forecasts: We project F3/27 OP of ¥490.8bn (guidance ¥430bn,
FactSet consensus ¥453.9bn), up ¥208.9bn YoY and above F3/22's record of ¥424.1bn.
That is because we expect profit in the Components segment to rise ¥186.2bn to ¥501.3bn
and losses in the Device & Modules segment to shrink by ¥24.1bn from -¥26.5bn to -

¥2.4bn. We look for Components profit to rise due to a +31.6% YoY increase in MLCC
sales in dollar terms (+36.1% on yen basis), a continued increase after F3/26’s +13.8% rise
(+12.6% on yen basis), product mix improvement, and higher utilization rates absorbing
increased depreciation and other fixed cost growth. We expect Device & Modules losses
to shrink mainly due to the falling out of ¥43.8bn in Resonant goodwill impairment and
¥6bn from the disposal of the micro battery business. We project F3/28 OP of ¥702.6bn

(consensus ¥655.3bn). The projected ¥211.8bn YoY growth breaks down as Components +
¥170bn and Devices & Modules +¥39.6bn. We expect Components profit to rise thanks to
a 24.5% YoY increase in capacitor (MLCC) sales in dollars (+23.7% on yen basis) and
continued product mix improvement absorbing increased depreciation & other fixed cost
growth. We look for Devices & Modules profit to rise on factors including increased sales
of high-frequency modules, secondary batteries, and vertical power delivery modules.

We project 2Q OP of ¥125bn (consensus ¥122.9bn). The projected QoQ ¥26.6bn growth
breaks down as Components +¥12.5bn QoQ to ¥126bn, and Devices & Modules +¥13.9bn
QoQ to ¥1bn. For Components, we expect sales of MLCCs for AI/data centers to continue
to increase, the MLCC product mix to improve, and utilization rates to continue to rise. For
Devices & Modules, we expect sales for North American smartphones to increase
seasonally and reenter the black, but because North American smartphone production is

split between fall models and spring models, profit contributions are likely to be limited.

Murata’s share price risk/reward: Even though we raise our earnings estimates for

Murata, we lower our DCF-based price target from ¥12,500 to ¥11,000, mainly reflecting
an increase in our DCF model’s risk-free rate from 2.60% to 3.0%. Murata's share price has
fallen 41% year-to-date from its June 22 peak, and we see 53% upside over the next 12-18
months, maintain our OW rating, and designate the stock as our Top Pick in Japan's
electronic components industry. The reasons are as follows: (1) We forecast the dollar-
based shipment value of MLCCs, where Murata held the top global share of 40% in 2025,

to expand at a CAGR of 21% through 2031, with Murata continuing to gradually gain
market share. (2) Demand for MLCCs used in AI servers/DCs is likely to continue
expanding, and demand for small, high-capacity, high-value-added products, which Murata
has brought to market ahead of competitors, should grow substantially. (3) Outside of
MLCCs, we look for demand to expand for small, high-value-added products, including
secondary batteries for DC BBUs, power modules for vertical power delivery in AI servers,

and RF modules for smartphones, and then expand even further as edge AI devices

### Page 18

M
Japan Insight
18
become widespread. (4) In addition to earnings growth, we expect that management’s
proactive approach to shareholder returns, including share buybacks, will raise capital
efficiency and lift ROE.
Bull case ¥14,000, 95% projected upside
In our bull case, we assume higher sales of MLCCs for AI servers/data centers than in our
base case, for component sales of 7% higher than our base case, for OP of ¥560.3bn in
F3/27, ¥794bn in F3/28, and ¥1.515tn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF

assumptions are the same as for our base case. Bull case fair value corresponds to 58.2x of
fully-diluted F3/27 EPS of ¥240.4, 41.1x fully-diluted F3/28 EPS of ¥340.7, and 21.1x fully-
diluted F3/31 EPS of ¥664.0.
Bear case ¥3,500
Murata products are used in all kinds of different electronic devices, and they are easily
affected by the macro economy and inventory cycle. In our bear case, we assume slower
global economic growth rates and lower production of high-end consumer goods like

high-end smartphones than in our base case for OP of ¥314.6bn in F3/27, ¥330bn in F3/28,
and ¥362.4bn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF assumptions are the same as in
our base case. Here we look for ROE to increase from 8.8% in F3/26 to 9.3% in F3/27 and
9.4% in F3/28, above capital cost of 7.5%. With profit below the market consensus, we
assume the share price falls to a P/B of 2.4x.

SEMCO earnings forecasts
Shawn Kim
AI-driven MLCC growth and pricing to drive sharp earnings
acceleration in FY27, with continued growth in FY28
FY26-28 forecasts: We project SEMCO OP of W2.34tn in FY26 (+156% YoY), W5.70tn in
FY27 and W8.36tn in FY28, implying further growth of 144% and 47%, respectively. We
expect the earnings acceleration to be driven mainly by the Components (MLCC) and
Package Solution businesses, while the Optics business remains a relatively modest

contributor. In FY27, our projected OP of W5.70tn breaks down as Components W4.56tn,
Package Solution W943bn, and Optics W201bn, versus W1.67tn, W510bn, and W165bn,
respectively, in FY26. In FY28, we forecast Components OP rising further to W6.55tn,
Package Solution to W1.58tn, and Optics to W230bn. As such, we expect SEMCO's consol.
OP margin to grow from 8.1% in FY25 to 15.7% in FY26, 24.5% in FY27, and 25.9% in FY28.
We maintain the stock as our top pick within our S. Korea Technology coverage.

For Components, we expect profit growth to accelerate as AI-server demand drives both
strong MLCC volume growth and a structurally richer product mix. High-end AI MLCC
supply remains tight, with lead times exceeding 20 weeks, supply-chain inventories lean
following two years of destocking, and book-to-bill at the three high-end suppliers
reaching its highest level since the pandemic. Importantly, contract pricing has increasingly
been institutionalized through long-term agreements, while the high-end AI MLCC market

remains concentrated, with Murata and SEMCO together holding around 85% share.
Capacity additions are also unlikely to close the supply-demand gap quickly, as most
meaningful new capacity is scheduled for 2027 or later and suppliers are generally
targeting only around 10-15% annual output growth. We thus expect tighter supply-
demand, higher pricing, and a shift toward high-capacitance, low-ESL AI products to drive
Components OP margin from 21.4% in FY26 to 30.9% in FY27 and 30.1% in FY28.

Bull case W3,000,000
Our bull case assumes ABF opportunities from major US CSPs drive stronger content
growth, with future ASIC content & volume growth continuing to outpace our base-case
expectations. Customer diversification toward non-mobile customers should also reduce
earnings volatility and improve profitability. Our bull case, equivalent to 15x FY27e P/B,
reflects what we see as an unprecedented super-cycle in the underlying AI-related MLCC

and ABF markets.
Bear case W1,350,000
In our bear case, we assume slower content growth in MLCCs and substrates due to
greater competition and China's localization, together with prolonged consumer weakness
weighing on the Optics business. However, we still expect increasing AI exposure to
structurally lift SEMCO's MLCC and ABF ASPs, product mix and margins, given the
difficulty commodity suppliers face in penetrating the premium market. Our bear-case

value corresponds to 7x FY27e P/B. Key downside risks include a significant pullback in

### Page 22

M
Japan Insight
22
Samsung's mobile flagship cycle, lower SEMCO market share, execution risk in penetrating
Chinese smartphone customers, and continued consumer-demand weakness.

Morgan Stanley MUFG 25
Yageo earnings forecasts
Howard Kao
Tightening MLCC Narrative is Unchanged
We forecast CY26 revenue of approximately NT$183B, up 38% y/y, driven primarily by
stronger AI-related demand, with a more modest contribution from consumer
applications. This demand strength should support broad-based utilization improvement
across Yageo’s passive component portfolio. Pricing is also turning more favorable, with
distributor price increases implemented in 2Q26 and Yageo raising prices for direct

customers in 3Q26, as previously highlighted. The combination of higher utilization and
firmer pricing should drive meaningful operating leverage. We forecast gross margin
expanding to 39.3%, up 310bps y/y, and operating margin reaching 27.4%, up 500bps y/y,
supporting EPS of approximately NT$19.79.
Looking into CY27, we expect supply/demand conditions in the mid- and low-end markets
to tighten further, sustaining pricing momentum and driving an additional improvement in

utilization. We therefore forecast revenue of approximately NT$252B, up 38% y/y.
Continued operating leverage should lift gross margin to 43.2%, an increase of 390bps y/y,
and operating margin to 33.4%, up 600bps y/y, resulting in EPS of approximately NT$32.11.
Why has the stock been so volatile?
Yageo benefited from a sharp increase in MLCC pricing in 2Q26, driven by a temporary
spike in spot-market demand. In our view, ODM/EMS customers accelerated inventory

replenishment amid concerns that the ramp in high-end AI-server MLCC demand,
particularly alongside Nvidia’s Vera Rubin platform, could constrain supply of mid- and
low-end MLCCs. Under normal market conditions, these customers typically carry only 3-4
weeks of inventory. However, supply concerns prompted a rapid increase in inventory
coverage to approximately 4-6 months.
During this restocking phase, ODM/EMS customers sourced MLCCs from both
manufacturers, including Yageo, and distributors. The resulting demand surge drove a

sharp increase in spot/distributor pricing and also contributed to the stock’s strong
performance. However, the pricing move was largely driven by a one-off inventory build.
Once customers had secured sufficient inventory, purchasing activity normalized and spot
pricing began to soften. This, together with weaker broader market sentiment, led to a
pullback of ~50% from the stock’s peak of approximately NT$1,140.
At the current share price, however, we believe the risk-reward has become attractive. The

underlying fundamentals, and, importantly, our view of the MLCC upcycle, remain intact.
Our analysis indicates substantial incremental demand for high-end, high-capacitance
MLCCs of 47µF and above, which will require a meaningful increase in dedicated
production capacity. We expect this to drive further capacity conversion toward high-end
MLCCs, with the resulting crowding-out effect becoming more visible from late CY26 into
early CY27. This should progressively tighten supply in the mid- and low-end MLCC

market and provide renewed support for pricing.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
We lower our 12-month price target to NT$1,050 (from NT$1,465): This reflects our
lower earnings estimates for CY26-28. Our price target is our base case value, derived
from our residual income valuation model. Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware
companies within our coverage, we think this methodology provides the most accurate

value of the firm by taking into account the cost of equity.
Our key assumptions are:
• Cost of equity of 7.8%:
° Risk-free rate of 1.0% (10-year Taiwanese government bond yield);
° Equity risk premium of 6.8%;
° Beta of 1.1;

### Page 27

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 27
• Medium-term growth rate of 12% (down from 16%);
• Terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our price target implies 33x 2027e P/E and 24x 2028e P/E. These are above its five-year
historical range of 8x to 18x, which we view as justified considering its longer-term
transformation story to become a global total solutions provider, moving from passive
into active components. In addition, compared to global passive component peers, we

believe Yageo stock remains undervalued with higher ROE and margins but lower P/E vs.
Japanese peers (which trade on an average at 40x+ P/E).
Our bull and bear case scenario values decrease by magnitudes similar to the change
to our base case value, to NT$1,490 and NT$480, respectively.

Taiyo Yuden earnings forecasts
We expect expect record profit in F3/29, not F3/28; upgrade
the stock from UW to EW
Full-term F3/27 and F3/28 earnings forecasts, F3/27 2Q forecasts: We project F3/27 OP
of ¥45.1bn (guidance ¥45bn, FactSet consensus ¥42bn), up +¥25.1bn YoY. We expect sales
in dollars of core product capacitors (MLCCs) to increase +22.6% YoY (+27.4% on yen
basis), a continued increase following on FY3/26’s +10.4% rise (+8.5% on yen basis), and

higher utilization rates absorbing increased depreciation expense and other fixed cost
increases. We project F3/28 OP of ¥66.1bn (consensus ¥73.1bn), up +¥25bn YoY. We look
for sales in dollars of capacitors (MLCCs) to rise +17.3% YoY (+16.6% on a yen basis),
absorbing increased depreciation expense and other fixed cost increases. We expect OP to
rise ¥83.8bn in F3/29 (consensus ¥107.1bn), surpassing the historical high profit of
¥68.2bn in F3/22.

We project 2Q OP of ¥12.6bn (consensus ¥11bn), up +¥7.8bn from ¥4.8bn in 1Q. We look
for MLCC sales to rise +18.1% compared with 1Q and for the utilization rate to rise from
85% in 1Q to 95% in 2Q. With increased sales of inductors for North American
smartphones, there should be a major profit increase on 1Q.

Morgan Stanley estimate vs. Consensus high/mean/low（Y bn）
Note: Consensus is based on FactSet; Source: Fact Set, Morgan Stanley Research
Taiyo Yuden share price risk/reward: We upgrade Taiyo Yuden from UW to EW, as we
believe its share price, having fallen 56.0% from its July 1 peak, has limited downside risk

and offers 6% upside over the next 12-18 months. We lower our DCF-based price target
from ¥12,500 to ¥10,500. This mainly reflects an increase in the DCF model’s risk-free
rate from 2.60% to 3.0%. We rate the stock EW rather than OW because: (1) In its core
MLCC business, we estimate Taiyo Yuden's MLCC market share was 11.3% in 2025, down
from 12.7% in 2020, and we think this downtrend continued into 2026, with its market
share shrinking to 10.7% in 1Q and 9.9% in 2Q. (2) We believe Taiyo Yuden is lagging

behind Murata (No. 1 share) and SEMCO (No. 2) in small, high-capacity MLCCs for AI
server accelerator boards, where demand is rapidly expanding. (3) We expect consensus
estimates for F3/28 onward to be revised down after 2Q results.
Bull case ¥14,000, 49% projected upside
In our bull case, F3/27 sales of MLCCs for AI servers/data centers are 7% higher than in our
base case, for OP of ¥56.5bn in F3/27, ¥85.5bn in F3/28, and ¥176.3bn in F3/31. Our forex

assumptions and DCF assumptions match our base case. Bull case fair value corresponds
to 50.7x our fully-diluted F3/27 EPS estimate of ¥276.1, 33.6x our fully-diluted F3/28 EPS
estimate of ¥417.0, and 15.9x our full-diluted F3/31 EPS estimate of ¥879.1.
Bear case ¥4,500
Taiyo Yuden’s care products (MLCCs and inductors) are used in all kinds of electronic
devices, and are easily affected by the macro economy and inventory cycle. In our bear
case, we assume slower global economic growth rates and lower production of high-end

consumer goods like high-end smartphones than in our base case, for OP of ¥39.2bn in
F3/27, ¥56.1bn in F3/28, and ¥65.8bn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF
assumptions are the same as in our base case. Here we look for ROE of 7.3% in F3/27 and
10.1% in F3/28, improving to a level above capital cost of 7.9%. With profit below market
expectations, we assume the share price falls to a P/B of 1.8x.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Delta Electronics
Add to APAC Key Call Buy - Taiwan Summit
reaffirms the AI Power opportunity
Capturing a robust industry TAM as the leading server power supplier
Delta affirmed a stronger growth profile as AI infrastructure now is over 50% of sales.
Our bottom-up model from our APAC Focus (link) estimates a 69% CAGR to US$91.1bn
AI server power TAM from 2025-2030 even moderating 2028-30 for hyperscale cash

flow and grid power constraints. We see a strong 2027 driven by +65% YoY accelerator
die growth, Rubin's ~60% higher rack power and AMD's move to rack-scale systems.
400V/800V becomes a larger driver in 2028-29 as rising power density outgrows low-
voltage distribution. We believe Delta is key beneficiary with its  broad grid-to-chip
portfolio helping sustain its ~40% share of a rising TAM.
Near-term supply constraints bottoming from Aug and accelerating into Q4.

Supply constraints on Power ICs have tempered Q3 growth from +15-20% QoQ to +10-
12% QoQ but should grow in Sep and accelerate to double digit QoQ in Q426 and
above seasonal Q127 (UBS +3% QoQ) into new ASIC/GPU platform ramps and more
supply. Management expects shortages to persist for at least the next several quarters
with demand accelerating faster than supply and the supply-demand gap likely
widening before narrowing but price hikes rebounding OpMs.

HVDC Adoption Appears Increasingly Inevitable.
HVDC production and shipments have begun and should be modest in 2027 before
ramping sharper in the following 1-2 years. As rack power rises from 140kW on GB300
to nearly 250kW on VR200 and potentially higher on Rubin Ultra, management expects
space and efficiency constraints to shift power systems out of racks and into side-car or
centralized architectures. Delta sees HVDC as the logical solution for power-loss,

thermal, and density challenges, with higher-voltage architectures offering multiple-
times higher value per watt than current solutions. Delta sees three hyperscalers
evaluating 400V, two adopting it for NVIDIA racks initially before converging on 800V.
Reiterate Buy and PT NT$2,750
Delta's stock has pulled back 36% since May following push-outs to NVIDIA's
architecture slowing the dedicated power rack ramp-up and elevating supply
constraints, pulling it back to 22x/17x 2026-27 EPS. We now see better entry with sales

ramping in H226, spec upgrades to ASIC power racks and Rubin at 60% higher TDP over
Blackwell and margins holding better than feared. The company should leverage its
strong product line, R&D and field support to participate in the key power architecture
transitions in the coming years moving the power stack out of the rack and also
developing emerging nascent technologies including SST for facility level power
transformation and rectification and fuel cells licensing Ceres for medium-term

opportunity to supplement the power grid constraint with alternative energy.

Forecast returns

Company Description
Delta Electronics provides computer switching power supply solutions, as well as video display
and electronics components, for computers, smartphones, telecom and industrial products.
Founded in 1971, the firm has production bases in Taiwan, China, Thailand and India. In

2023, its power electronics, infrastructure and automation segments contributed 62%, 25%
and 13% of revenue, respectively.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target on PE multiple valuation.

Delta's businesses include SPS and components for a variety of end-applications, video
displays and data networking subsystem and system products. Any changes in these end-
markets could affect our forecasts for Delta. Investment risk could increase/decrease on any
deviation from our expectations for end-market demand and competition.
Downside risks for Delta include: severe supply chain constraints; weaker-than-expected PC/
server demand; a slower-than-expected IA recovery; and a slower ramp of 5G in 2022.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the

### Page 1

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Voltronic Power
UBS Taiwan Summit 2026 key takeaways:
Earnings growth hinges on UPS/HVDC deliveries
Sales turning to growth in H226; sales mix change driving margin expansion
Management indicated bookings and shipments remained solid in July-Aug despite
temporary PCB shortages, and it expects a sales turnaround to growth in H226. Inverter
sales could stay weak on competition and Voltronic's product portfolio adjustments

(reducing low-margin products). Meanwhile, increasing outsourcing by brands will
support UPS sales growth. Management expects HVDC products to start contributing in
2027. Voltronic added a third customer for HVDC products. We think a higher sales
portion from UPS/HVDC products and inverter product adjustments could bring GM
expansion in 2027.
UPS growth intact;  HVDC and PDU represent the next legs of growth
The company expects sales to resume growth by single digits  in H226  and expects >10%

YoY growth in 2027. Management remains cautious about solar  inverters due to intense
competition from Chinese peers and expects inverter sales to decline as Voltronic adjusts
its product portfolio, focusing more on commercial solar  and premium products. For
2027, management expects offline UPS to grow 0-5%, supported by outsourcing
opportunities and emerging market demand, which could offset notebook weakness.
High-power UPS could account for an increasingly higher sales portion, driven by

accelerating outsourcing by tier 1 customers. For HVDC, management highlighted it
obtained one more client. HVDC product progress is on schedule, with 800VDC power
shelf sampling expected by year-end, a pilot run in H127 and mass production in H227.
The company is preparing testing and production equipment at factories in Taiwan and
China. Over the longer term, management also sees opportunities in PDUs, a market it
estimates at US$5bn in 2025, with a 12-15% sales CAGR over the next 5-8 years.

Margin  improvement on product mix optimisation and higher prices
Voltronic has implemented two UPS price increases this year to offset higher raw
material costs, which it expects to be reflected on its P&L starting in Sept 2026. For solar
inverters, management plans to focus on higher power and premium products with a
GM above 20%, while reducing low-power products with a GM at or below 20% over
the next two to three quarters, which could support GM expansion. In upcoming

quarters, solar inverter products may shrink to <20% of the total sales mix, while higher-
margin businesses such as high-power UPS and HVDC products continue to ramp. We
think the sales mix change will expand margins in 2027.
Valuation: Maintain Buy rating and PT of NT$1,128
We maintain our Buy rating and price target of NT$1,128, based on sum-of-the-parts
analysis, applying 28x/23x/24x 2027E PE for UPS/inverter/PSU business, respectively.

Company Description
Founded in 2008, Voltronic is a leading ODM for uninterruptible power supply (UPS) and
inverters. Its major customers include Schneider, Vertiv, and Riello. Voltronic is headquartered
in Taiwan and has production plants in mainland China, Vietnam, and Taiwan.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement

We base our price target on sum of the parts.
We believe the key risks for Voltronic include: 1) a slower-than-expected outsourcing trend
from leading branded UPS companies; 2) a faster-than-expected ramp-up of the electricity
grid in developing countries resulting in lower-than-expected demand for off-grid solar
systems; 3) FX risks; and 4) rising raw material prices.
Key industry-specific risks include: 1) unexpected changes in the underlying global

macroeconomic environment; 2) currency and interest rate movement; and 3) commodity
price inflation.

past 12 months.
KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 3

We see several indicators suggesting strong ABF equipment demand, including (1) the 
lead time for ABF vacuum laminators has extended into 2031, implying ~1.5k units of 
mid-to-high end equipment orders (assuming UTR is full for Eternal Precision’s capacity 
in both Japan and China throughout 2027-31), vs. ~600 units of total equipment 
shipped during 2022-25. This suggests demand for ABF equipment is strengthening 
(even stronger than previous upcycle in 2020-22) and shifting toward higher-end 
(management mentioned that the four major Taiwanese ABF customers mainly ordered 
the 90-ton 3-stage vacuum laminators for new capacity expansions), (2) Eternal 
Precision has seen some customers voluntarily offer to pay a premium to secure 
equipment supply and shorten the long lead time, a trend that was not observed by the 
company during the last upcycle, and (3) contract liabilities uptrend has just started 
(Exhibit 2), and we expect to see even stronger growth in coming years considering the 
upcoming ABF substrate industry capacity expansion trend (from 2027 to at least 2031, 
Exhibit 3) 
The extended order lead time for the key ABF equipment supports our positive view on 
the ABF substrate industry. ABF suppliers remain proactive in capacity expansion, but 
demand continues to outpace supply, thus we expect the shortage ratio of ABF 
substrates could reach 14%/34%/51% in 2H26/2027/2028 (see here). We see limited 
risk from capacity expansion by China ABF suppliers due to their limited access to new 
ABF equipment. Eternal Precision has seen some China ABF customers offering 
premiums to shorten equipment lead time, but the company mentioned that they 
prioritize major customers, as well as those with secured ABF film supply (we continue to 
see limitations on ABF film supply for China ABF suppliers). Meanwhile, more 
end-customers are asking key ABF suppliers for capacity beyond 2029, consistent with 
the fact that major ABF suppliers have better access to key ABF equipment. 
In terms of future substrate products, Unimicron previously mentioned that MP 
shipment of EMIB-T substrates could start in 2027 (see here). While Japanese ABF 
suppliers will be the first to have access to the new ABF vacuum laminators for EMIB-T 
substrates given their better production yields, Unimicron is tracking close by targeting a 
50% yield for this new product. For the more advanced glass core substrates, we 
continue to see Kinsus and Unimicron making progress with this new technology (see 
here and here), while commercialization is unlikely in the near term (beyond 2029 per 
GSe). Overall, we believe that the most advanced packaging substrates could become a 
key revenue/GM driver in the longer term given their better pricing/margin profile (most 
advanced packaging substrates typically have GM of 60%+ upon MP vs. 
Kinsus/Unimicron GM at 26.1%/24.8% as of 2Q26). 
Maintain our Buy ratings on NYPCB (on CL) and Kinsus, but stay Neutral on Unimicron, 
with unchanged 12m TPs for NYPCB/Kinsus/Unimicron of 
NT$2,500/NT$1,125/NT$1,220.  
Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy-rated on NYPCB 
16 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Taiwan ABFFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 27/41% in 2026/27E) due to the
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 1H25), we believe the expanding
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s

high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (122%

2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s
earnings and stock performance.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,500 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)

Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.
Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~106% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 2H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,125 for Kinsus is based on

16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We are Neutral rated on the
shares given long-term growth opportunity prospects: (1) over 70% of ABF shipments
are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and makes
price hike less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker than

peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its ability to
capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing more
favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see better pricing outlook
to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF account for 10% of the
company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers due to the
company continuing to lose market share to peers given its less favourable production

yield and slow capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our
outlook for limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally
positive view in the long term on its profitability trajectory.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,220 for Unimicron is
based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E

(10.3x).
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

2H26 先進製程動能延續、ABU 復甦，展望正向 
預期 3Q26 大客戶先進製程需求維持強勁、SBU、DBU 新增產能且達滿載，
ABU 受惠傳產機械/工具機業需求回溫及部份產品調漲價格等，預期營收季
增 8%、毛利率在產品組合好轉、規模經濟效益下，估計季增 0.5~1 個百分
點達 40.7%、EPS 3.83 元/季增 10%。展望 4Q26 延續先進製程需求強勁，
其中鑽石碟營收逐月新高，SBU 優化產品組合及特殊晶圓佔比提升而估計毛
利率趨勢向上，推估營收季增 1%、EPS 3.96 元/季增 3%。 
先進製程有助產品內容價值提升，趨勢有利 
2Q26 ABU 、DBU、SBU 營收佔比分別為 25%、31%、43%，其中鑽石碟
來自最大客戶先進製程佔比已達 63%，N3/N2 佔比皆為 22%，並且 1.4nm
小量出貨，對鑽石碟、再生晶圓的內容價值，包括 N2 對 N3 CMP 內容價值
升 10~15%，尤其 A16 CMP 道數由 55 提升至 77 道，內容價值進一步提升
至 2~3 成。再生晶圓 N3/N2 每投片 10 萬片對再生晶圓需求約 2~3 倍，趨勢
有利後續營運。延伸 ABU、DBU 技術，公司亦投入減薄砂輪研發，主要因
應大客戶先進封裝/晶背供電、本土化策略等需求，有機會突破 Disco 壟斷局
面，惟目前仍為產品驗證階段，短期效益不大。 
DBU、SBU 供不應求，訂單能見度至 2030 年 
DBU 目前規劃產能年底至 7 萬顆，根據大客戶 N2/先進封裝產能倍增，加上
來自美系記憶體/IDM 廠潛在需求，預計 27 年產能達 9~10 萬片，中長期目
標>20 萬片。SBU 擴產提前至 2Q26 達 40 萬片滿載，新增以高階再生晶圓
為主，有助毛利率改善。 ABU 來自傳產/工具機需求回溫及子公司合併綜效，
使得營收成長>2 成、毛利率達 25%水準。元大最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 分
別為 14.23/20.85 元，上修 7%/11%，基於近幾季毛利率表現優於預期，主
要來自產品組合改善，包括先進製程佔比提升、再生晶圓產能擴充且以高階
產品為主及 ABU 復甦及等，使得毛利率優於我們先前預估 2~3 個百分點。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
預期 2H26 DBU 成長維持強勁、 SBU 新增產能效益、 ABU 需求回
溫，營收、毛利率趨勢向上。 
⧫  
展望 2026~27 年隨著主力客戶擴大 N3/N2 產能及海外廠佈局，推升鑽
石碟、再生晶圓供不應求，搭配新產能，營運具更佳的槓桿效應。 
⧫  
預估 2026/27 年 EPS 14.23/20.85 元，分別上修 7%/11%。維持買進
評等，40 倍目標本益比、2027 年 EPS 評價基礎、反映先進製程展望
佳、能見度達未來 2~3 年及佈局減薄砂輪為下一個觀察重點。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 9 月 17 日 Kinik 第3 頁，共 16頁  
先進製程有助產品內容價值提升，趨勢有利 
2Q26 ABU 、DBU、SBU 營收佔比分別為 25%、31%、43%，其中鑽石碟來自最大客戶先進製程
佔比已達 63%，N3/N2 佔比皆為 22%，並且 1.4nm 小量出貨，對鑽石碟、再生晶圓的內容價值，
包括 N2 對 N3 CMP 內容價值升 10~15%，尤其 A16 CMP 道數由 55 提升至 77 道，內容價值進一
步提升至 2~3 成。再生晶圓 N3/N2 每投片 10 萬片對再生晶圓需求約 2~3 倍，趨勢有利後續營運。
延伸 ABU、DBU 技術，公司亦投入減薄砂輪研發，主要因應大客戶先進封裝/晶背供電、本土化策
略等需求，有機會突破 Disco 壟斷局面，惟目前仍為產品驗證階段，短期效益不大。 
DBU、SBU 供不應求，訂單能見度至 2030 年 
DBU 目前規劃產能年底至 7 萬顆，根據大客戶 N2/先進封裝產能倍增，加上來自美系記憶體/IDM
廠潛在需求，預計 27 年產能達 9~10 萬片，中長期目標>20 萬片。SBU 擴產提前至 2Q26 達 40 萬
片滿載，新增以高階再生晶圓為主，有助毛利率改善。 ABU 來自傳產/工具機需求回溫及子公司合
併綜效，使 得營收成 長 >2 成、毛利率達 25%水 準。元大最新 預估 2026/27 年 EPS 分別為
14.23/20.85 元，上修 7%/11%，基於近幾季毛利率表現優於預期，主要來自產品組合改善，包括
先進製程佔比提升、再生晶圓產能擴充且以高階產品為主及 ABU 復甦及等，使得毛利率優於我們
先前預估 2~3 個百分點。 
傳統砂輪製造跨足至半導體製程及晶圓再生產業 
中砂原為專業的砂輪製造廠，近年由於傳統產業轉型之需要，轉往從事衍生性鑽石製品的研發創新
及進行企業改造，並跨足高科技晶圓再生產業，公司優勢在於客製化，因為自有切割設備，可以做
非標準厚度的晶圓，相較而言，國際大廠很難因為少量訂單做規格更改。目前中砂在再生晶圓及鑽
石碟業務方面取得了顯著的成績，市占率分別達 15-20%及 20-25%，為該領域的全球領先者之一。  
圖 3：2Q26 營收占比 (部門別) 圖 4：產品營收占比

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、Bloomberg、元大投顧 
晶圓代工客戶 3nm 製程產能提升帶動鑽石碟銷售增加 
中砂在鑽石碟技術已取得顯著進展，過去中砂在 5nm 製程的產品市佔率低於 5 成，但進到 3nm 製
程後市占率提升至 7 成，超越國際大廠 3M，整體產品已能夠在中砂完成各式測試後交貨給客戶，
大幅提升產品品質、效率，滿足客戶需求。目前隨台積電 3nm 製程產能逐步增加，預估中砂鑽石
碟出貨量將隨之成長。未來中砂在台積電 N2 鑽石碟供貨比將有望拉升至 8 成以上，技術優勢逐漸
顯現，且製程進入 2nm 後，對於鑽石碟所要求的品質提高、消耗量增加，先進製程鑽石碟內涵價
值平均增加約 5-10%、且用量也會增加，將對中砂鑽石碟產品的價量有正面影響，帶動其鑽石碟業
務之動能。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 9 月 17 日 Kinik 第4 頁，共 16頁  
圖 5：2Q26 鑽石碟銷售比例(單一最大客戶) 圖 6：2Q26 鑽石碟營收季增率

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 
晶背供電帶動鑽石碟、再生晶圓內容價值進一步提升，IDM 具成長潛力 
為了達到晶背供電的技術，晶圓薄化為關鍵角色，需將晶圓打磨到足夠薄之後，才可實現對電晶體
直接供電，此舉將會使 CMP 需求提升，先進製程鑽石碟市占率極高的中砂為潛在受惠者。 
中砂之大客戶先進製程持續往 N3/N2 推進，CMP 研磨層數更為增加，對鑽石碟規格要求提升及使
用量亦增加，估計 N3 轉 N2 對公司鑽石碟的內容價值提升約 1-2 成，亦使得公司在大客戶供應比
持續提升，過往公司在 N5 鑽石碟對台積電供應比<5 成，N3 提升至 7 成，預期 N2 將達 8 成，已
開始小量出貨。除了台積電以外，Intel 目前多款鑽石碟在驗證中。

圖 8：再生晶圓用量隨製程微縮而增加
